The protein is destined for the secretory pathway, and the signal peptide is cleaved.<br>- **D**: Chloroplast transit peptides are longer, rich in serine/threonine, and lack the strong hydrophobic core seen here.<br><br>The evidence overwhelmingly supports option C.<br><br>2. answer: C</td>

2. Loop entropy: larger loops have more conformational entropy → bonds with very distant cysteines are less stable (entropic penalty)

- Cys58-Cys68: ring size = 68-58 = 10
